# RCX Task List (Canonical)

This file is the single source of truth for authorized work.
If a task is not listed here, it is NOT to be implemented.

---

## North Star (Keep This True)

1. RCX VM is not a "runner". It is a substrate where **structure is the primitive**.
2. "Code = data" means execution is graph/mu transformation, not host-language semantics.
3. **Stall → Fix → Trace → Closure** is the native engine loop; everything else must serve it.
4. Closures/gates must be **explicit, deterministic, and measurable** (fixtures + replay).
5. Emergence must be attributable to RCX dynamics, not "Python did it".
6. Host languages are scaffolding only; their assumptions must not leak into semantics.
7. Buckets (r_null / r_inf / r_a / lobes / sink) are **native routing states**, not metaphors.
8. Seeds must be minimal (void/empty) and growth must be structurally justified.
9. Determinism is a hard invariant: same seed + rules ⇒ same trace/fixtures.
10. A "program" is a pressure vessel: seed + allowable gates + thresholds + observation outputs.
11. Enginenews-like specs are target workloads to prove: "does ω/closure actually emerge?"
12. Every task must answer: "Does this reduce host smuggling and increase native emergence?"
13. **L3 Parity: Python and JavaScript must run identical projections with identical semantics.**
    - Same seeds: kernel.v1, match.v2, subst.v2, recurrence.v1, recurrence.v2, exhaustion.v1, fix.v1, hemispheres.v1, rcx_engine.v1, metabolization.v1, bootstrap_structural.v1, terminal_classify.v1, metabolize_cycle.v1 (projection counts verified by `mu/tests/structural/test_seed_counts.py::EXPECTED_COUNTS`)
    - Same bootstrap primitives: eval_step, max_steps, stack_guard, projection_loader (mu_equal DEMOTED — Level 1 Content-Addressed Mu)
    - Any change to Python projection behavior MUST be mirrored in JS
    - Any new seed MUST be loaded and tested in BOTH substrates
14. **Seeds must declare their execution layer.** Every seed is either:
    - **BOOTSTRAP**: Runs via eval_seed.step() only (Python/JS substrate provides non-linear pattern support)
    - **META-CIRCULAR**: Runs via step_kernel_mu (kernel.v1 + match.v2 + subst.v2)
    - If a seed claims META-CIRCULAR, tests MUST verify it through step_kernel_mu
    - Non-linear pattern seeds become META-CIRCULAR only when bridge-backed structural runtime is default
    - Current BOOTSTRAP seeds: eval.v1 (uses [] arrays, requires bootstrap match/substitute)
    - Current META-CIRCULAR seeds: kernel.v1, match.v2, subst.v2, recurrence.v1, exhaustion.v1, classify.v1
15. **True self-hosting is the path.** The goal is structural computation without host semantics:
    - **L1 (Algorithmic)**: match/subst algorithms as Mu projections ✓ DONE
    - **L2 (Operational)**: kernel loop as Mu projections ✓ DONE (with bootstrap primitive acceptance)
    - **L3 (Substrate Portability)**: same projections run on Python AND JavaScript ✓ DONE
    - **L4 (True Self-Hosting)**: eliminate bootstrap primitives OR make them substrate-independent
    - Programs must run accurately and structurally, without host semantics (except irreducible bootstrap)
    - Tests must verify the CLAIMED execution path, not just correctness
    - If tests pass via bootstrap but fail via meta-circular kernel, that's a real bug (not theater)

---

## Governance (Non-Negotiable)

- Do not add new subsystems, execution models, or architectures without explicit approval.
- Do not create "new tests" to bypass a failing test; fix the failing test or the code.
- Do not leave broken files/tests behind and add replacements.
- Minimize file creation. Prefer editing existing files.
- v1 replay semantics are frozen. Any new observability must be v2 and gated.
- **L3 Parity Rule**: Changes to `mu/host/python/rcx_pi/selfhost/` (or via symlink `rcx_pi/selfhost/`) or `mu/` MUST be mirrored in `mu/host/js/eval_step.js`.
  - Run `node mu/host/js/eval_step.js` to verify all JS tests pass
  - Run `./tools/checks/check_js_debt.sh` to verify JS debt markers match Python
  - Run `./tools/checks/linters/contraband_js.sh` to verify no forbidden patterns (determinism, purity)
  - Run `./tools/checks/linters/ast_police_js.sh` to catch JS patterns that bypass grep
  - Run `./tools/checks/check_test_theater_js.sh` to catch vacuous JS assertions
  - Run `./tools/checks/linters/seed_police.sh` to verify seed integrity and no host leakage
  - New seeds must be loaded in both Python and JavaScript
  - Parity vectors must pass on both substrates before merge
- **Pre-commit doc review**: Before committing changes to `rcx_pi/` or `mu/`:
  1. Read relevant docs in `mu/docs/` (e.g., EVAL_SEED.v0.md, DeepStep.v0.md)
  2. Update docs if implementation differs from spec
  3. Update TASKS.md status if completing/progressing items
  4. Verify JS parity if projection behavior changed
- **Agent runbook**: Agent usage follows `mu/docs/agents/AgentRunbook.v0.md` (trigger map, gate rules, and evidence requirements)
- **Roadmap rule**: `ROADMAP.md` and docs in `roadmap/` define SEQUENCE and DESIGN only.
  - Current state lives in STATUS.md; authorization lives in TASKS.md
  - Gate completion updates TASKS.md (Ra/NEXT/VECTOR), not roadmap docs
  - Draft specs live in `roadmap/`; approved specs migrate to `mu/docs/core/`
  - See `roadmap/MANIFEST.md` for reading order and linking rules

## Collaboration Protocol (Default Working Mode)

- **VECTOR mode (design/creation)**: Explore ontology-to-runtime mappings, propose alternatives, and identify missing mechanisms. No implementation begins in VECTOR.
- **NEXT mode (execution/verification)**: Implement bounded, testable slices only. Mirror Python/JS semantics and preserve deterministic behavior.
- **Falsifiability gate**: Every new design claim must map to a measurable runtime behavior and at least one concrete test criterion.
- **Cross-model continuity**: Inputs from Codex, Claude, or Gemini are design references, not authority. Canonical authority remains `STATUS.md` + `TASKS.md`.
- **Promotion discipline**: Creative proposals must be explicitly promoted (`VECTOR -> NEXT`) before code changes.

---

## Promotion Criteria (Non-Negotiable)

- **SINK → VECTOR**: Item must have a clear semantic question to answer. A design doc must be written before any implementation. Promotion must be explicit and documented in this file.
- **VECTOR → NEXT**: Design doc must be complete and reviewed. Semantics must be locked. Implementation scope must be bounded and testable. Observability must precede mechanics.
- Promotion is never implicit. Moving an item between sections requires updating this file with rationale.
- **PR rule**: Any PR that implements a VECTOR/SINK item without an explicit promotion note in this file must be rejected.
- No implementation work may begin on VECTOR items. VECTOR is design-only.
- No SINK item may advance without answering: "What semantic question does this resolve?"

**Terminology Lock:** `sink` (lowercase) = runtime hemisphere bucket (e.g., `r_sink` in projection routing). `SINK` (uppercase) = governance task lane (this section, parked work items). `r_a` = runtime accumulator bucket. `Ra` = resolved-work section (below). These are distinct concepts; never conflate them.

---

- Tracker sync note (2026-05-06, commit-continuation-persisted-handoff-fix-2026-05-06): **PIPELINE REPAIR - bind commit continuation to persisted handoff.** Class: L4_ENABLER. target_gate_id: G8. evidence_command: `PYTHONHASHSEED=0 python3 -m pytest -q mu/tests/tools/test_commit_executor_receipt.py::TestSupervisorReceiptIsAuthority::test_standalone_empty_handoff_receipt_skips_provenance_check mu/tests/tools/test_commit_executor_receipt.py::TestReceiptChainEndToEnd::test_commit_packet_truth_refresh_binds_continuation_to_persisted_handoff -vv --tb=short && PYTHONHASHSEED=0 python3 -m pytest -q mu/tests/tools/test_executor_dispatch.py::TestDispatcherFreshnessRefresh::test_dispatch_stops_completed_bounded_candidate_before_tracker_update mu/tests/tools/test_executor_dispatch.py::TestModularSurfaceEntrypoints::test_phase_b_surface_stops_completed_bounded_packet_before_executor mu/tests/tools/test_executor_dispatch.py::TestModularSurfaceEntrypoints::test_commit_surface_stops_completed_bounded_packet_before_executor mu/tests/tools/test_executor_dispatch.py::TestRoutingRecordBuilderCompletedPacketRejection::test_status_predicate_keeps_pending_commit_routable mu/tests/tools/test_executor_dispatch.py::TestRoutingRecordBuilderCompletedPacketRejection::test_accepts_implemented_pending_commit_control_plane_packet -vv --tb=short && PYTHONHASHSEED=0 python3 -m pytest -q mu/tests/tools/test_commit_executor_receipt.py --tb=short && PYTHONHASHSEED=0 python3 -m pytest -q mu/tests/tools/test_executor_dispatch.py --tb=short`. evidence_delta: (1) commit_executor rekeys Phase B continuations after Step 5c persists the refreshed Phase B handoff. (2) commit_executor rekeys Phase B continuations again after Step 7 adds supervisor receipt evidence immediately before final durable handoff persist and continuation write. (3) Standalone continuations keep the original caller handoff hash because no refreshed Phase B handoff is persisted for standalone reruns. (4) Regressions now cover both the persisted .agent_bus/executors/phase_b_handoff.json rerun path and the standalone caller-handoff rerun path. (5) Dispatcher completed-packet stops now apply to UPDATE_TRACKER_ONLY and recoverable Phase B/commit surfaces before executor launch. (6) `IMPLEMENTED ... PENDING COMMIT` packet statuses remain routable until commit completion. progress_proof_before: A docs wave local commit could not resume after pre-push failure because the continuation record kept the pre-refresh handoff hash while the durable Phase B handoff carried refreshed evidence, so rerun fell back to Step 4 and failed with nothing staged; the first repair would have shifted the same mismatch onto standalone reruns; the completed-packet guard did not yet cover recoverable surface subcommands or UPDATE_TRACKER_ONLY; the status predicate treated pending-commit packets as already complete. progress_proof_after: Phase B continuation records bind to the exact durable handoff that later dispatcher/CLI reruns load, while standalone continuation records remain bound to the original standalone caller handoff that reruns provide; completed bounded packets now stop before direct dispatch tracker updates and before Phase B/commit surface executor launch, while pending-commit implemented packets stay routable. FOUNDER_OVERRIDE:commit-continuation-persisted-handoff-fix-2026-05-06. primary_blocker_class: INTEGRATION. primary_invariant_id: INV_STRUCTURAL_FORWARD_MOTION. indicator_artifact_ref: reports/l4_wave_indicators/commit-continuation-persisted-handoff-fix-2026-05-06.json. indicator_collection_command: python3 mu/tools/metrics/collect_l4_wave_indicators.py --wave-id commit-continuation-persisted-handoff-fix-2026-05-06 --output reports/l4_wave_indicators/commit-continuation-persisted-handoff-fix-2026-05-06.json. bootstrap_endgame_policy: SUBSTRATE_INDEPENDENT_MINIMAL_BOOTSTRAP. boot0_track_id: V1. boot0_progress_state: HOLD.

- Tracker sync note (2026-05-06, post-merge-package-next-queue-refresh-2026-05-06): **PIPELINE REPAIR - refresh post-merge package from next open queue packet.** Class: L4_ENABLER. target_gate_id: G8. evidence_command: `PYTHONHASHSEED=0 python3 -m pytest -q mu/tests/tools/test_commit_executor_post_merge_cleanup.py --tb=short && PYTHONHASHSEED=0 python3 -m pytest -q mu/tests/tools/test_executor_dispatch.py::TestDispatcherFreshnessRefresh::test_auto_refresh_rejects_stale_post_merge_package_before_supervisor --tb=short && PYTHONHASHSEED=0 python3 -m pytest -q mu/tests/tools/test_commit_executor_receipt.py --tb=short && python3 -m py_compile mu/tools/executors/commit_executor.py`. evidence_delta: (1) commit_executor writes a fresh post_merge_package.json after successful merge verification. (2) The package selector scans the founder-ordered remediation queue in TASKS.md and skips packets whose tracked packet Status is complete. (3) The selector writes the next open tests/tooling packet as the bounded candidate and writes an empty-candidate hard-stop package when the next open queue packet is /mu structural. (4) If every queued packet is complete, the selector overwrites stale package state with a current empty-candidate package instead of leaving a completed task routable. progress_proof_before: After PR #886, .agent_bus/meta/post_merge_package.json still carried merge_sha 38f8747f and still selected the already-completed docs non-blocking packet; executor_dispatch.py refuses stale package auto-refresh when package merge_sha differs from current HEAD. progress_proof_after: Future successful commit merges mechanically refresh the post-merge package against current HEAD and the next open founder-ordered queue packet, preventing stale completed packet replay and preserving the /mu hard-stop boundary; when no open packet remains, the package is refreshed to an empty-current queue-complete state. FOUNDER_OVERRIDE:post-merge-package-next-queue-refresh-2026-05-06. primary_blocker_class: INTEGRATION. primary_invariant_id: INV_STRUCTURAL_FORWARD_MOTION. indicator_artifact_ref: reports/l4_wave_indicators/post-merge-package-next-queue-refresh-2026-05-06.json. indicator_collection_command: python3 mu/tools/metrics/collect_l4_wave_indicators.py --wave-id post-merge-package-next-queue-refresh-2026-05-06 --output reports/l4_wave_indicators/post-merge-package-next-queue-refresh-2026-05-06.json. bootstrap_endgame_policy: SUBSTRATE_INDEPENDENT_MINIMAL_BOOTSTRAP. boot0_track_id: V1. boot0_progress_state: HOLD.
- Tracker sync note (2026-05-06, post-merge-completed-pending-status-predicate-fix-2026-05-06): **PIPELINE REPAIR - completed status beats pending detail.** Class: L4_ENABLER. target_gate_id: G8. evidence_command: `PYTHONHASHSEED=0 python3 -m pytest -q mu/tests/tools/test_commit_executor_post_merge_cleanup.py --tb=short && PYTHONHASHSEED=0 python3 -m pytest -q mu/tests/tools/test_executor_dispatch.py::TestRoutingRecordBuilderCompletedPacketRejection --tb=short && python3 -m py_compile mu/tools/executors/executor_common.py`. evidence_delta: (1) Shared packet-status classification now treats explicit `COMPLETED`, `LANDED`, and `CLOSED` statuses as terminal before evaluating pending-detail text. (2) `IMPLEMENTED ... PENDING COMMIT` remains routable until commit completion. (3) Post-merge package refresh has a regression for `COMPLETED (commit-ready, pre-commit supervisor pending)` audit packets so completed audit packets cannot be selected ahead of queued remediation packets. progress_proof_before: After PR #887 merged, Step 15b refreshed `.agent_bus/meta/post_merge_package.json` to the completed docs audit packet because `packet_status_is_completed` let the `pending` detail inside `COMPLETED (commit-ready, pre-commit supervisor pending)` override the explicit completed status. progress_proof_after: Completed audit/status packets with pending-detail wording are skipped by the shared status predicate, so the post-merge package selector continues to the next open founder-ordered remediation packet. - Tracker sync note (2026-05-06, merge-pr-null-graphql-sweep-guard-2026-05-06): **PIPELINE REPAIR - null-safe merge_pr.sh post-merge sweep.** Class: L4_ENABLER. target_gate_id: G8. evidence_command: `PYTHONHASHSEED=0 python3 -m pytest -q mu/tests/tools/test_executor_dispatch.py::TestMergePrSweepCount --tb=short && bash -n mu/tools/hooks/merge_pr.sh`. evidence_delta: (1) `merge_pr.sh` treats null/missing GitHub GraphQL `reviewThreads.nodes` as an empty thread list during bot resolution, human-thread counting, and sweep-finding extraction. (2) `merge_pr.sh` treats null/missing GitHub GraphQL PR `comments.nodes` as an empty top-level comment list during informational Codex-comment description. (3) The dispatcher test harness now exercises a fake merged PR whose GraphQL thread/comment containers are null and requires a clean sweep without `Cannot iterate over null`. progress_proof_before: PR #888 merged, but commit executor failed before Step 15b package refresh with `.agent_bus/observability/pipeline_agent_events.jsonl:2553` reporting `merge_pr.sh failed: jq: error (at <stdin>:1): Cannot iterate over null (null)`; the hook directly iterated nullable GraphQL arrays. progress_proof_after: A null/incomplete GitHub GraphQL sweep response is mechanically treated as no actionable threads, so a successful merge is not converted into a failed commit-executor outcome before post-merge package refresh. Packet: `reports/control_plane/pr912_commit_retry_state_closeout_repair_2026-05-09.md`. evidence_command: `PYTHONHASHSEED=0 python3 -m pytest -q mu/tests/tools/test_commit_executor_receipt.py::TestReceiptChainEndToEnd::test_commit_retry_pending_state_restored_before_receipt_review mu/tests/tools/test_commit_executor_receipt.py::TestReceiptChainEndToEnd::test_commit_retry_pending_state_demotes_again_when_receipt_validation_fails mu/tests/tools/test_commit_executor_receipt.py::TestReceiptChainEndToEnd::test_commit_retry_pending_state_demotes_again_when_handoff_persist_fails --tb=short && python3 -m py_compile mu/tools/executors/commit_executor.py mu/tests/tools/test_commit_executor_receipt.py && python3 mu/tools/checks/linters/check_private_attr_access.py mu/tests/tools/test_commit_executor_receipt.py`. evidence_delta: (1) PR #913 review found the first closeout repair restored retry-demoted packet/TASKS state before the supervisor receipt chain validated, while retry demotion was only armed for post-validation failures; the next supervisor pass found the remaining fallible handoff-persist gap after restoration. (2) `mu/tools/executors/commit_executor.py` now restores retry-demoted state before final supervisor receipt review, records `restore_commit_retry_state` immediately after restoration succeeds before durable handoff persistence, records the restored handoff sha, rekeys the continuation when safe, and treats that step as the arming point for later failures before `git_commit`. (3) Regression proof demotes packet/TASKS back to `IMPLEMENTED - PIPELINE REPAIR PENDING COMMIT` when receipt validation or handoff persistence fails after restoration, and proves `restore_commit_retry_state` precedes `build_and_run_supervisor` and `validate_receipt` on the success path. progress_proof_before: a Step 6, early Step 7, or post-restoration handoff-persist failure after the first repair could leave completed packet/TASKS state without a new commit. progress_proof_after: post-restore pre-commit and handoff-persist failures are demoted again before dispatcher re-entry, while successful receipts cover the final restored staged content. This is control-plane retry-state narrowing only and does not add Python/JS host runtime semantics. FOUNDER_OVERRIDE:pr912-commit-retry-state-closeout-repair-2026-05-09.

- Tracker sync note (2026-05-12, js-engine-pipeline-shape-governance-test-2026-05-12): **NEXT-CODEX-POST-REDTEAM - JS engine pipeline shape governance structural guard.** Class: L4_ENABLER. Category: /mu structural governance. target_gate_id: G8. Packet: `reports/control_plane/js-engine-pipeline-shape-governance-test-2026-05-12_2026-05-12.md`. structural_artifact_ref: `mu/tests/structural/test_engine_pipeline_discipline.py`. evidence_command: `PYTHONHASHSEED=0 python3 -m pytest -q mu/tests/structural/test_engine_pipeline_discipline.py::TestJsEnginePipelineShapeGovernance::test_dependency_direction_and_boundary_authority --tb=short && ./tools/checks/check_docs_consistency.sh && python3 tools/checks/enforce_l4_execution_contract.py --staged --wave-id js-engine-pipeline-shape-governance-test-2026-05-12`. evidence_delta: (1) The focused structural guard locks current JS engine dependency direction: routing may depend on pipeline/kernel, pipeline may depend on kernel/core, and kernel remains downstream of core only. (2) Expensive Boot1 default/parity classes now stay in owned slow lanes with source-lock tests selected by the fast shard; slow L4 default evidence remains merge-blocking. (3) Scheduler parity Node timeout moved from 30s to 60s after local focused proof measured a scheduler case at 31.90s. (4) Commit executor Step 8b now mirrors the PR fast-shard marker filter and deterministic `ci_fast` env after the first resend timed out at 960s by running all affected slow-marked files serially. (5) Host-semantics and host-authority ratchets remain unchanged; this repair does not alter runtime semantics. progress_proof_before: test-only CI repair was rejected by the commit supervisor when packaged under the existing L4_STRUCTURAL runtime wave because the staged package had zero runtime/substrate files, and the first L4_ENABLER commit resend reached supervisor COMMIT_GO but timed out in Step 8b. progress_proof_after: the repair has an explicit L4_ENABLER packet, tracker note, source-lock coverage, same-PR control-surface authorization, and commit-gate fast-shard routing without relabeling the runtime wave. (2) executor_common.apply_role_agents extracted from _materialize_role_agents (behavior-identical) so the runtime loader and the CLI share one derivation rule; DEFAULT_EXECUTOR_CONFIG.role_agents.reviewer flipped claude to codex with backends set to the materialized fixed-point. (3) executor_config.json role_agents set to implementer=claude reviewer=codex with consistent derived fields; one brittle alignment assertion de-brittled to consistency-based; new test_set_roles.py covers write/switch/idempotency/validation/env-shadow; test_growth_caps.py CAP_TEST_FILES 123->124 and CAP_TOOL_SCRIPTS 48->49 bumped +1 each with FOUNDER_OVERRIDE notes for the two added files. progress_proof_before: reviewer=codex was forced only by RCX_BRIDGE_REVIEWER_OVERRIDE in settings.json, silently shadowing the committed role_agents that declared reviewer=claude; no single switch existed. progress_proof_after: committed executor_config.json role_agents is the single source (reviewer=codex); set_roles flips roles self-consistently; 18 alignment plus 8 set_roles plus 523 dispatch plus 1692 collateral plus 119 bridge/autoping tests pass; tmux/dashboard labels verified to render the effective role. - Tracker sync note (2026-06-01, implementer-post-result-exit-timeout-2026-06-01): **NEXT-CODEX-POST-REDTEAM -- bridge adapter post-result exit-timeout (implementer finished-but-alive hang hardening).**. Class: L4_ENABLER. target_gate_id: G8. evidence_command: `PYTHONHASHSEED=0 python3 -m pytest -x --tb=short mu/tests/tools/test_agent_bridge_supervisor.py -k post_result`. evidence_delta: (1) bridge_adapters: new opt-in post_result_exit_timeout_s on run_adapter / _run_adapter_buffered / _run_adapter_streaming. The per-line tee callback detects the claude stream-json terminal {"type":"result"} event (new helper _line_is_terminal_result_event, top-level type only) and, once the grace elapses with the process still alive, kills it and RETURNS the captured output instead of raising. (2) phase_b_implementer.invoke_implementer reads timeouts.phase_b_implementer_post_result_exit (default DEFAULT_IMPLEMENTER_POST_RESULT_EXIT_TIMEOUT_S=120s) and passes it. (3) Three regression tests in test_agent_bridge_supervisor.py prove buffered + streaming kill-and-return on a lingering post-result process and that a clean exit within the grace is unaffected. Full bridge suite 92 + phase_b_executor 424 pass (no regression).. progress_proof_before: The implementer adapter's only post-completion backstop was the stale watchdog (_start_stale_watchdog) whose _progress_fingerprint counts whole-process-tree accumulated CPU with no ceiling, so a finished-but-alive claude --print whose child (e.g. an MCP server) accrues background CPU never goes stale; the wave hung to the wall-clock spec.timeout_s, which then raised a timeout and DISCARDED the already-emitted success result envelope (observed as the #28 tier-3 short-circuit / wave failure).. progress_proof_after: Once the adapter emits its terminal result the teardown window is bounded by post_result_exit_timeout_s (default 120s): a lingering subprocess (hung Stop hook / MCP teardown) is killed and the completed result is returned and preserved for the implementer to consume, instead of being discarded as a timeout. Opt-in (implementer only); reviewer / meta adapters that pass no timeout are unchanged. Unblocks #28.. FOUNDER_OVERRIDE:implementer-post-result-exit-timeout-2026-06-01. primary_blocker_class: INTEGRATION. primary_invariant_id: INV_STRUCTURAL_FORWARD_MOTION. indicator_artifact_ref: reports/l4_wave_indicators/implementer-post-result-exit-timeout-2026-06-01.json. indicator_collection_command: python3 mu/tools/metrics/collect_l4_wave_indicators.py --wave-id implementer-post-result-exit-timeout-2026-06-01 --output reports/l4_wave_indicators/implementer-post-result-exit-timeout-2026-06-01.json. bootstrap_endgame_policy: SUBSTRATE_INDEPENDENT_MINIMAL_BOOTSTRAP. boot0_track_id: V1. boot0_progress_state: HOLD.

- Tracker sync note (2026-06-03, post-merge-verify-root-validation-2026-06-03): **NEXT-CODEX-POST-REDTEAM -- _resolve_post_merge_verify_root validates the linked base-branch worktree (dir exists + same common git dir + on base_branch) before use; falls back to repo_root when stale/missing, so a dead nightly-ci-repair worktree can't flip an already-merged PR's post-merge verify to Status:error or trigger a spurious pr_conflicting/unknown_error recovery cascade; only that resolver + private helpers; regression coverage in existing test_commit_executor_post_merge_cleanup.py + updated existing post-commit-continuation mocks in test_executor_dispatch.py.**. Class: L4_ENABLER. target_gate_id: G8. Packet: `reports/control_plane/post_merge_verify_root_validation_2026-06-03.md`. evidence_command: `PYTHONHASHSEED=0 python3 -m pytest -x --tb=short mu/tests/tools/test_commit_executor_post_merge_cleanup.py mu/tests/tools/test_executor_dispatch.py`. evidence_delta: _resolve_post_merge_verify_root now validates the linked base-branch worktree (exists + same common git dir + on base_branch) before returning it; stale/missing falls back to repo_root. Existing post-commit-continuation tests in test_executor_dispatch.py updated to mock the new git probes (--show-toplevel, --git-common-dir, --abbrev-ref) so they pass under the stricter validation. Prevents the post-merge verify 'fatal: not a git repository' on a dead linked worktree (standalone Status:error; dispatcher spurious pr_conflicting->unknown_error cascade) on an already-merged PR.. progress_proof_before: _resolve_post_merge_verify_root returned the linked worktree from _find_linked_worktree_for_branch without validating it exists / is the live linked worktree for this repo+branch, so a stale nightly-ci-repair worktree caused 'fatal: not a git repository' (PR #1064/#1065 standalone; #32/#1070 dispatcher spurious tier2->tier3 cascade).. progress_proof_after: The resolver validates the linked worktree (exists + same common git dir + on base_branch) and falls back to repo_root when stale/missing; happy paths unchanged; covered by regression tests in test_commit_executor_post_merge_cleanup.py and updated mocks in test_executor_dispatch.py.. - Tracker sync note (2026-06-03, adapter-return-on-complete-envelope-2026-06-03): **NEXT-CODEX-POST-REDTEAM -- bridge_adapters.run_adapter returns the captured output on a post-result non-zero adapter exit (when result_terminal_seen_at is armed = implementer + a terminal result event was seen) instead of raising 'exited N' and discarding completed work, eliminating the wasted implementer re-invoke (#43); implementer-scoped (reviewer/meta keep raise-on-nonzero); 2 regression tests into existing test_agent_bridge_supervisor.py.**. Class: L4_ENABLER. target_gate_id: G8. Packet: `reports/control_plane/adapter_return_on_complete_envelope_2026_06_03.md`. evidence_command: `PYTHONHASHSEED=0 python3 -m pytest -x --tb=short mu/tests/tools/test_agent_bridge_supervisor.py -k "exits_nonzero_after_terminal_result or raises_on_nonzero_exit_without_terminal_result"`. evidence_delta: bridge_adapters.run_adapter (_run_adapter_streaming + _run_adapter_buffered) now returns the captured output on a non-zero exit when a terminal result event was seen (result_terminal_seen_at armed = implementer), instead of raising 'exited N' and discarding completed work -- so a stop-hook-error non-zero exit after the implementer's result no longer forces a wasted re-invoke. Implementer-scoped (reviewer/meta unchanged). 2 regression tests added to the existing test_agent_bridge_supervisor.py (verified: the fix-confirming test fails on the un-fixed code with 'exited 1').. progress_proof_before: run_adapter raised BridgeAdapterError 'exited N' on a non-zero exit even when the adapter had emitted its terminal result event, discarding the captured output -> phase_b classified the round failed and re-invoked the implementer (a wasted round on every Phase B wave whose implementer hit a stop-hook-error exit; #43, observed on #32/#37/#36).. progress_proof_after: On a non-zero exit with a terminal result event seen (implementer), run_adapter returns the captured output; the downstream result-subtype check classifies success/error -> a completed implementer round advances instead of re-invoking. Reviewer/meta adapters keep raise-on-nonzero. Covered by 2 regression tests in the existing test_agent_bridge_supervisor.py.. - Tracker sync note (2026-06-08, supervisor-run-wave-evidence-trusted-v8-2026-06-05): **NEXT-CODEX-POST-REDTEAM -- #52 v8: pre-commit supervisor runs the wave's declared evidence_command pre-merge via bash -c then snapshots/restores the worktree (tracked + untracked) AND the git index to pre-evidence state, verifies compute_repo_state is unchanged, so staged content + anti-tamper guard + pre-commit receipt are unaffected and a mutating evidence (incl. the indicator collector) is harmless. Gate outside is_cs_wave: fail-closed-when-declared (provided -> run+restore; declared-but-omitted -> fail-closed; not-declared -> skip); false/omitted -> NEEDS_PHASE_B. BOTH commit_executor AND phase_b_executor pass evidence_command (4-file). meta_bridge_supervisor.py + commit_executor.py + phase_b_executor.py + regression in existing test_meta_bridge_supervisor.py. NOTE: this wave's OWN evidence_command is a structural grep proof (non-self-referential) because #52 is self-referential -- any pytest of the supervisor suite run inside the supervisor re-enters its lock/state (tracked as #54); the full 152/152 suite runs in CI..**. Class: L4_ENABLER. target_gate_id: G8. Packet: `reports/control_plane/supervisor_run_wave_evidence_trusted_v8_2026-06-05.md`. evidence_command: `grep -q _run_wave_evidence_with_restore mu/tools/agents/meta_bridge_supervisor.py && grep -q evidence_command mu/tools/executors/phase_b_executor.py && grep -q evidence_command mu/tools/executors/commit_executor.py`. evidence_delta: Adds a trusted, fail-closed-when-declared wave_evidence runner to the pre-commit supervisor that runs the evidence_command via run_validation_command(['bash','-c', ..]) then snapshots/restores the worktree (tracked + untracked) AND the git index and verifies compute_repo_state is unchanged, so a mutating evidence command is harmless to staged content/receipt. Gate runs outside is_cs_wave; provided -> run+restore; declared-but-omitted -> ValidationResult False; not-declared -> skip; false/omitted -> all_passed False -> NEEDS_PHASE_B. BOTH commit_executor and phase_b_executor pass evidence_command. This wave's evidence_command is a structural grep proof (verifies _run_wave_evidence_with_restore + the evidence_command pass-through are wired) -- chosen because #52 is self-referential; the full 152/152 suite passes in CI (follow-up #54 restores the full-suite dogfood once the runner is re-entrant).. progress_proof_before: The supervisor never executes the wave's declared evidence_command (commit_executor only embeds it as text), so a wave can pass review and merge green without its claimed proof ever running.. progress_proof_after: The supervisor runs the wave's declared evidence_command pre-merge (trusted bash -c) then restores the worktree so the run is side-effect-free; a failing/omitted proof reroutes to Phase B (NEEDS_PHASE_B) instead of merging, with staged content + receipt + anti-tamper guard intact.. - Tracker sync note (2026-06-08, wave-evidence-lock-propagate-2026-06-08): **NEXT-CODEX-POST-REDTEAM -- wave-evidence-lock-propagate: fix the #52 fail-closed P2 (PR #1082 bot). _run_wave_evidence_with_restore (meta_bridge_supervisor.py) now RE-RAISES the _MetaBridgeLock MetaBridgeError on lock contention so it propagates to run_meta_bridge_package's wait_for_lock_seconds retry loop (wait+retry) instead of catching it as a failed wave_evidence gate -> false NEEDS_PHASE_B; other evidence/restore errors still become a gate failure (the unlocked runner handles its own errors). test_evidence_restore_is_guarded_by_supervisor_lock asserts propagation (pytest.raises) + evidence not run. Scope = 2 files: mu/tools/agents/meta_bridge_supervisor.py + mu/tests/tools/test_meta_bridge_supervisor.py..**. Class: L4_ENABLER. target_gate_id: G8. Packet: `reports/control_plane/wave_evidence_lock_propagate_2026-06-08.md`. evidence_command: `grep -q 'wait_for_lock_seconds retry loop can' mu/tools/agents/meta_bridge_supervisor.py && grep -q 'Lock contention must PROPAGATE' mu/tests/tools/test_meta_bridge_supervisor.py`. evidence_delta: Re-raises the lock MetaBridgeError from _run_wave_evidence_with_restore so concurrent supervisor runs wait+retry (run_meta_bridge_package's wait_for_lock_seconds) instead of being falsely routed to NEEDS_PHASE_B; other evidence/restore errors still become a gate failure. Updated test_evidence_restore_is_guarded_by_supervisor_lock to assert propagation + that the evidence command did not run. Verified: 152 supervisor tests + audit_fast green. (Structural grep evidence_command because the supervisor's own wave_evidence runs it during this commit and a pytest of the supervisor suite would re-enter -- same non-self-referential pattern as #52.). progress_proof_before: A concurrent supervisor holding meta_bridge.lock made the wave_evidence runner return a failed gate (lock message) -> NEEDS_PHASE_B, falsely routing a healthy wave to Phase B (PR #1082 deferred P2).. progress_proof_after: Lock contention propagates as a MetaBridgeError to run_meta_bridge_package's retry loop, which waits and retries; only genuine evidence/restore failures route to a gate failure / NEEDS_PHASE_B.. Verified empirically: the fixed helper ff'd the live main repo addf2151 -> 0b079ed0 with 3 untracked files present; 30 post-merge-cleanup tests pass incl. a new regression (test_sync_primary_ffs_with_untracked_files_present asserting untracked-present -> synced=True + untracked preserved) and the dirty-skip test updated to TRACKED dirt.. progress_proof_before: The #51 auto-ff-sync skipped ('is dirty; not clobbering WIP') whenever the founder's main repo held any untracked file, so it drifted behind dev after standalone/dispatcher merges and had to be ff'd manually.. progress_proof_after: The auto-ff-sync runs after every merge even with untracked files present (blocks only on tracked WIP); the founder's main repo stays current with dev automatically.. ## Boundary Question (Answered)

What is the smallest, host-independent execution primitive that RCX must possess
such that a structural program can cause new structure to emerge only via
Stall → Fix → Trace → Closure, and in no other way?

**Answer:** The Structural Reduction Loop (MATCH → REDUCE/STALL → TRACE → NORMAL_FORM).
See `archive/docs/MinimalNativeExecutionPrimitive.v0.md` for invariants and non-goals.

Teammate worktree integration is implemented by code: `mu/tools/executors/executor_dispatch.py:578-605` adds `phase-a --teammate-lane`, `:1430-1555` rejects default/shared bus roots, stale worktree HEADs, dirty caller worktrees, unregistered target paths, and active bridge locks, and `:1558-1590` binds the configured lane to the teammate worktree plus namespaced bus before dispatch. - **[P2]** Checkpoint/Resume Contract (bounded continuation semantics) — Explicit pause/resume semantics when engine or algorithm limits are hit. **Opened** (2026-02-14, Round 16E). **Founder decision D2=DEFER** (2026-02-16): not promoted; remains in VECTOR for future consideration. **Invariants**: (1) Limit hit is explicit (no silent truncation), (2) Hash verifies state integrity; hash alone is not resumable state, (3) Resume token carries full continuation state (or validated pointer), state_hash, seed/version checksums, and budget metadata, (4) Resume path preserves Python/JS parity, (5) Resume path reuses existing validation/security guards, (6) No bootstrap primitive count increase without explicit approval. **Promotion criteria (all required for VECTOR → NEXT):** (1) Contract doc drafted and approved, (2) Canonical token schema defined, (3) Cross-substrate parity test plan defined, (4) Security review complete, (5) Explicit VECTOR → NEXT promotion in this file with rationale.
- **[P3]** Content-Addressed Mu (`roadmap/ContentAddressedMu.md`) - Every Mu value carries a content hash; equality becomes O(1). **Levels 0-2 IMPLEMENTED** (L0: boundary hashing, L1: mu_equal eliminated 5→4, L2: frozen hashes — state dropped from _seen, ~77% memory savings). **Level 3 (Trie) DEFERRED** — analysis shows 5x slower for production traces (<50 steps), break-even at ~100 steps. Revisit if traces routinely exceed 100 steps.
- **[P4]** Projection Indexing - Preprocess projections into structural trie/decision-tree for O(log N) matching instead of O(N) linear scan. Index is Mu data (structural). **Promotion criteria:** Profile real workloads first; if projection matching is >50% of runtime, promote to NEXT. **Profiling plan (2026-03-02):** Measure `_match_inner`/`match` as fraction of `run_engine_pipeline` end-to-end time on canonical cycling workload (A↔B, 2 projections, 10 steps, 20 engine iterations, 100 algorithm iterations).
  - Python: `PYTHONHASHSEED=0 python3 - <<'PY'` with `cProfile` harness: `from rcx_pi.selfhost.step_mu import run_engine_pipeline; run_engine_pipeline([{"id":"c.l","pattern":{"state":"A"},"body":{"state":"B"}},{"id":"c.l","pattern":{"state":"B"},"body":{"state":"A"}}], {"state":"A"}, max_steps=10, max_engine_iterations=20, max_algorithm_iterations=100, use_boot1_recursive=False)` — inspect `pstats.Stats(pr).sort_stats("cumtime").print_stats(40)`.
  - JS: `node --cpu-prof mu/host/js/eval_step.js --json-api '{"action":"run_engine_pipeline","projections":[{"id":"c.l","pattern":{"state":"A"},"body":{"state":"B"}},{"id":"c.l","pattern":{"state":"B"},"body":{"state":"A"}}],"input":{"state":"A"},"maxSteps":10,"maxEngineIterations":20,"maxAlgorithmIterations":100}'` — load `.cpuprofile` in Chrome DevTools or `speedscope`. **Note:** `--json-api` dispatch bypasses the human CLI self-tests; run `node mu/host/js/eval_step.js` without `--json-api` when profiling self-test startup.
  - **Go/no-go rule:** GO P4 only if projection matching/selection hotspot is >50% of end-to-end runtime on measured workloads. If ≤50%, explicitly defer P4, record measured percentages, and set re-evaluation trigger (re-measure when projection count exceeds 50 or new seed files double current corpus).
  - **Measurement results (2026-03-02) — P4 DEFERRED with measured evidence:**
    - Python canonical (2 projs, cycling A↔B): `_match_inner` cumtime = 2.376s / 4.696s = **50.6%**; tottime (self-only) = 0.886s = **18.9%**. 94.6% of 272k match attempts are NO_MATCH (failures P4 could skip). P4-addressable savings estimate: **47.8%** (below threshold).
    - Python secondary (8 projs, 7 inputs): `_match_inner` cumtime = 2.676s / 5.900s = **45.4%** (below threshold).
    - JS canonical (engine subtree only, self-tests excluded): `match()` = 143.2ms / 362.2ms = **39.5%** (below threshold).
    - Dominant non-matching overhead: `is_mu`/`assert_mu` validation (Python 36-41%), `muHashCached` stall detection (JS 16.2%).
    - **Decision: DEFER.** Cumtime-based 50.6% is borderline but inflated by inclusive counting of operations any implementation needs (isinstance, dict.keys). The P4-addressable fraction (failed match elimination) is 47.8%. Secondary workload and JS are clearly below 50%. Re-evaluation trigger unchanged: projection count >50 or seed corpus doubles from current 19 files.
